# Remaining panicking stubs

Generated 2026-10-24 from the sources of `rust/*/src` (excluding `raw/` and `c2r/`) at the commit that added this file. A *stub* is a callee declared by `hook!` / `lb_hook!` / `ch_hook!` / `stub!` / `ext_fns!` / `oext_fns!` / `hooks!` / `todo_fns!` / `todo_ext!` / `todo_cb!` in a TU's `mod ext` (unset hook: `panic!("TODO: <name> is not ported")`), an explicit `Some(f) => f(..), None => panic!` function (`tu_ext.rs`), or a function whose whole body is `todo!` / `panic!` / `unimplemented!`. Stubs whose unset hook falls back to a port (`hook_or!`, `None => crate::tu::..`, `stub_or!`, the three resolve passes) are not listed.

A *site* is one (file, name) declaration; the same C function is stubbed once per TU that calls it, so the number of distinct names is much smaller than the number of sites.

## Tags

| tag | meaning |
|---|---|
| `NO_PORT` | no Rust function of that name (case-insensitive) with a real body exists in any crate. |
| `SIGNATURE` | a port exists in the stub's own crate or in a crate it depends on, but the stub was not rewired (different parameter/return types, arity, or the port takes references / `&mut self`). Candidates for a typed adapter. |
| `CYCLE` | a port exists only in a crate that cannot be depended on from the stub's crate (the stub's crate is below it in the dependency order, or the two are siblings). Needs a trait / hook layer or moving the port down. |

Crate order (a crate may depend on the ones to its left): `melee_core`, `melee_types`, `melee_runtime`, `melee_world`, `melee_items`, `melee_fighter`, `melee_chara`, `melee_modes`; `melee_audio` and `melee_net` sit beside them (`melee_audio` -> runtime, `melee_net` -> fighter), `melee` is the binary.

Method: `scratchpad` scripts (rsmod/stubs/classify, not committed) index every `fn` with a body, blank comments and strings, and resolve macros per file (a macro counts as panicking if its definition in that file, else in that crate, contains `panic!` / `todo!` / `unimplemented!`).

Per-crate figures count a name once per crate and tag; a name can be stubbed in several crates (the last cell counts distinct names overall).

## melee_chara

No panicking stubs remain (was NO_PORT 164 sites / 92 names, SIGNATURE 149 / 91, CYCLE 6 / 5; 2026-10-24, branch `port/fill-melee_chara`).

- SIGNATURE: every stub is a `port_hook!` (hook, unset = the port) over a typed adapter in `melee_chara/src/adapt.rs`, or a direct call; the Kirby copy-ability callbacks (28) and the `chars/cb.rs` entries call the character ports.
- NO_PORT: ported where the C file lives: `melee_fighter::tu::{ftcoll, ftmaterial, ftCo_09f4, ftparts_render (ftparts.c)}`, `melee_runtime::tu::{jobj_ptr (jobj.c on raw pointers), mex_data, os_report}` and `random::GLOBAL`, `melee_world::cm::camera_boss` (camera.c) and `NativeEfHost` (efasync.c), `melee_chara::tu::{ftsamus, ftsamusspeciallw1, ftsamusspecialn, ftcrazyhandcancel, ftcrazyhandcapturewaitcrazyhand, ftcrazyhandfingerbeam}`.
- CYCLE: moved below `melee_modes` and re-exported there: `melee_runtime::tu::{script_hit (ScriptGame_HitRuleDamage/Special/Forget + the capsule table), gmvs_state (gm_8016B1D8 and the VS controller global)}`, `melee_fighter::script_caps` (ScriptGame_CapsMaxJumps and its entity lookup); `HSD_JObjGetMtxPtr` is `jobj_ptr`'s.
- Not stubs (kept): `blaster_assert` in `tu/ftkirbyspecialfox.rs` is the C `HSD_ASSERT` failure path.
- Callees that these ports reach and that are still hooks live in the lower crates' lists (GX matrix loads and `HSD_TExp*` in `melee_runtime`, `grLib_801C9CEC` in the camera, the `EfHost` services without a native port, the m-ex/skins tables until the host installs them).

## melee_world fill status

Counts against the original melee_world section (813 names / 1842 sites): NO_PORT fixed 84, remaining 87; SIGNATURE fixed 391, remaining 69; CYCLE fixed 65, remaining 117. Total fixed 540, remaining 273 (plus 41 names the original list missed, 314 names / 470 sites in all).

Tools: `rust/melee_world/gen/rewire/rewire.py` (+ports.txt) rewires stubs; `rust/melee_runtime/gen/pure/run_pure.py <stem>` builds shipping-contraction C oracles (EXTRACT headers supported).
Verified natively against C: mtx_pc, cobj_world, jobj_world, gobj_native (state oracle), os_error, spline_world, mtx_world, itacc, gmmain (about 115 functions). Adapters over earlier verified ports are covered by the crate suites only.
Merge notes: tu/globals.rs is byte-identical to the runtime sibling's; the runtime sibling may also port GObj/JObj/CObj raw.

Remaining by reason:
